```python
import cv2
import numpy as np

# Load the cascade classifier for face detection
face_cascade = cv2.CascadeClassifier(cv2.data.haarcascades + 'haarcascade_frontalface_default.xml')

# Open the camera (if you have one)
cap = cv2.VideoCapture(0)

while True:
    # Capture frame-by-frame
    ret, frame = cap.read()

    # Convert the frame to grayscale
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)

    # Detect faces in the grayscale frame
    faces = face_cascade.detectMultiScale(gray, 1.1, 2)

    # Draw rectangles around the detected faces
    for (x, y, w, h) in faces:
        cv2.rectangle(frame, (x, y), (x+w, y+h), (255, 0, 0), 2)

    # Display the resulting frame
    cv2.imshow('Face Detection', frame)

    # Press 'q' to exit
    if cv2.waitKey(1) & 0xFF == ord('q'):
        break

# Release the camera and close all Open

Resentment can be caused by various factors, such as past conflicts, hurtful words or actions, or perceived injustices.

In relationships, resentment can manifest in different ways, including:

* Feeling angry or resentful towards the person who wronged you
* Feeling hurt or betrayed by the person's actions or words
* Feeling like you've been taken advantage of or used
* Feeling like you've been ignored or dismissed
* Feeling like you've been treated unfairly or unappreciated

1. **Detection methods**: Astronomers use various methods to detect exoplanets, including:
   - Transit method: measuring the decrease in brightness of a star as a planet passes in front of it.
   - Radial velocity method: measuring the star's wobbling motion caused by the planet's gravitational pull.
   - Direct imaging: capturing images of the planet directly.
